 487:     private PaperPosition completeClose(
 488:             PaperPosition position,
 489:             BigDecimal exitPrice,
 490:             PositionStatus status,
 491:             String closeReason,
 492:             String explanation,
 493:             TradeSignal exitSignal
 494:     ) {
 495:         BigDecimal remainingPnl = exitPrice.subtract(position.getEntryPrice(), MC)
 496:                 .multiply(position.getQuantity(), MC);
 497:         // FIX-11T: preserve any P&L already realized by a Near-TP partial harvest.
 498:         BigDecimal pnl = (position.getRealizedPnl() == null ? BigDecimal.ZERO : position.getRealizedPnl())
 499:                 .add(remainingPnl, MC);
 500: 
 501:         position.setExitPrice(exitPrice);
 502:         position.setRealizedPnl(pnl);
 503:         position.setStatus(status);
 504:         position.setCloseReason(closeReason);
 505:         position.setExitReason(explanation);
 506:         position.setExitSignal(exitSignal);
 507:         position.setClosedAt(Instant.now());
 508:         PaperPosition saved = positionRepository.save(position);
 509: 
 510:         // FIX-020: all terminal exits (TP/SL/SIGNAL_SELL/manual/etc.) create an immutable
 511:         // evidence boundary. Progressive adds remain untouched while the position is open.
 512:         executionIntelligenceService.completePositionOpportunity(position.getSymbol(), exitSignal, closeReason);
 513: 
 514:         if (exitSignal != null) {
 515:             try {
 516:                 // FIX-028: keep execution behavior identical, but preserve the TRUE terminal
 517:                 // reason in wallet history. A TAKE_PROFIT/STOP_LOSS may use the latest 1m
 518:                 // signal as its price/context carrier even when that signal is WATCH. Calling
 519:                 // executeSell(...) used to mislabel those exits as SIGNAL_SELL.
 520:                 if ("SELL".equalsIgnoreCase(closeReason) || "STRONG_SELL".equalsIgnoreCase(closeReason)) {
 521:                     walletAutoExecutionService.executeSell(exitSignal);
 522:                 } else {
 523:                     walletAutoExecutionService.executeSignalLinkedExit(exitSignal, closeReason, explanation);
 524:                 }
 525:             } catch (RuntimeException ex) {
 526:                 log.error("Automatic wallet exit failed for signal {}: {}", exitSignal.getId(), ex.getMessage(), ex);
 527:             }
 528:         }
 529: 
 530:         // FIX-028: immutable production audit records the actual close trigger separately
 531:         // from the latest market signal. This is diagnostic only and cannot alter execution.
 532:         productionExitAuditService.record(saved, exitSignal, closeReason, explanation);
 533:         return saved;
 534:     }

  69:         onPrice(symbol,price);
  70:         // No-position and all successful no-op exits reach this statement; exceptions do not.
  71:         priceEvents.finish(eventId,symbol,observedAt,price,"APPLIED","PROTECTION_CALLBACK_COMPLETED");
  72:     }

 391:     private void closePaper(String symbol, BigDecimal exitPrice, PositionStatus status,
 392:                             String closeReason, String explanation, TradeSignal sourceSignal) {
 393:         PaperPosition paper = paperPositionRepository.findBySymbolAndStatus(symbol, PositionStatus.OPEN).orElse(null);
 394:         if (paper == null) return;
 395:         BigDecimal remainingPnl = exitPrice.subtract(paper.getEntryPrice(), MC).multiply(paper.getQuantity(), MC);
 396:         // FIX-11T: paper_position may already contain realized P&L from a Near-TP partial
 397:         // harvest. Preserve it when the remaining quantity is later closed.
 398:         BigDecimal pnl = (paper.getRealizedPnl() == null ? BigDecimal.ZERO : paper.getRealizedPnl())
 399:                 .add(remainingPnl, MC);
 400:         paper.setExitPrice(exitPrice);
 401:         paper.setRealizedPnl(pnl);
 402:         paper.setStatus(status);
 403:         paper.setCloseReason(closeReason);
 404:         paper.setExitReason(explanation);
 405:         paper.setClosedAt(Instant.now());
 406:         PaperPosition saved = paperPositionRepository.save(paper);
 407: 
 408:         // FIX-028: live-price exits (TP/SL/Profit Lock/normal exit) also write the
 409:         // immutable audit record. sourceSignal is context only unless the closeTrigger
 410:         // itself is a genuine SELL decision. No protection/exit policy is changed here.
 411:         productionExitAuditService.record(saved, sourceSignal, closeReason, explanation);
 412:     }

===== src/main/java/com/crypto/audit/service/ProductionExitAuditService.java:25-65 =====
  25: @Service
  26: @RequiredArgsConstructor
  27: public class ProductionExitAuditService {
  28:     private final ProductionExitAuditRepository auditRepository;
  29:     private final WalletManagedPositionRepository managedPositionRepository;
  30:     private final PositionAnalysisRepository positionAnalysisRepository;
  31: 
  32:     @Transactional
  33:     public void record(PaperPosition paper, TradeSignal sourceSignal, String closeTrigger, String explanation) {
  34:         if (paper == null || paper.getId() == null || paper.getExitPrice() == null || closeTrigger == null) return;
  35:         if (auditRepository.findTopByPaperPositionIdOrderByAuditedAtDesc(paper.getId()).isPresent()) return;
  36: 
  37:         WalletManagedPosition managed = paper.getSignal() == null ? null : managedPositionRepository
  38:                 .findTopByEntrySignalIdOrderByOpenedAtDesc(paper.getSignal().getId()).orElse(null);
  39:         PositionAnalysis analysis = managed == null || managed.getId() == null ? null : positionAnalysisRepository
  40:                 .findTopByWalletPositionIdOrderByAnalyzedAtDesc(managed.getId()).orElse(null);
  41: 
  42:         auditRepository.save(ProductionExitAudit.builder()
  43:                 .paperPositionId(paper.getId())
  44:                 .walletPositionId(managed == null ? null : managed.getId())
  45:                 .symbol(paper.getSymbol())
  46:                 .closeTrigger(closeTrigger)
  47:                 .sourceSignalId(sourceSignal == null ? null : sourceSignal.getId())
  48:                 .sourceSignalDecision(sourceSignal == null || sourceSignal.getDecision() == null ? null : sourceSignal.getDecision().name())
  49:                 .sourceSignalOriginalDecision(sourceSignal == null || sourceSignal.getOriginalDecision() == null ? null : sourceSignal.getOriginalDecision().name())
  50:                 .positionAnalysisId(analysis == null ? null : analysis.getId())
  51:                 .positionRecommendation(analysis == null || analysis.getRecommendation() == null ? null : analysis.getRecommendation().name())
  52:                 .entryPriceUsdt(paper.getEntryPrice())
  53:                 .exitPriceUsdt(paper.getExitPrice())
  54:                 .stopLossUsdt(paper.getStopLoss())
  55:                 .takeProfitUsdt(paper.getTakeProfit())
  56:                 .closeExplanation(explanation)
  57:                 .auditedAt(Instant.now())
  58:                 .build());
  59:     }
  60: }

# FIX-154 ƒ?" Implementation Handoff for Code Review

Date: 2026-10-10  
Status: implemented in the local working tree; not deployed.

## Scope

FIX-154 changes only higher-timeframe BUY authority. It does not enable or
change FIX-153 reconciliation/runtime behavior.

The objective is to distinguish trusted, strong bearish 1h evidence from
unknown or weaker evidence without changing a stored historical signal.

## Implemented policy

1. The current 1m BUY still requires fresh 5m and 1h context.
2. A bearish 5m returns the existing `5M_BEARISH_VETO` first.
3. A bearish 1h with `data_quality_valid` `NULL` or `false` returns
   `1H_BEARISH_VETO`.
4. A trusted 1h `STRONG_SELL` with `confidence_score >= 70` returns
   `HTF_TRUSTED_BEARISH_VETO`.
5. A trusted 1h `SELL`, or `STRONG_SELL` with confidence below 70, is a
   SOFT conflict. It passes effective 1h `NEUTRAL` to profile logic and caps
   every allowed initial entry at 25%.
6. A non-bearish 1h with unknown quality keeps its raw decision; FIX-154 does
   not tighten bullish/non-bearish authority merely because historical quality
   is absent.
7. Existing progressive/add logic retains its raw 1h requirement. A SOFT
   conflict cannot authorize an add. Optional `SOFT_HTF_ADD_BLOCKED` evidence
   is observational only.

## Database changes

`V107__fix_154_trusted_higher_timeframe_buy.sql` adds:

- `trade_signal.data_quality_valid BOOLEAN NULL`.
- Replay parity columns on `trade_signal_test`:
  `btc_context_candle_close_time`, `btc_context_age_seconds`,
  `btc_context_freshness_threshold_seconds`, `signal_mode`, and
  `data_quality_valid`.
- Append-only `buy_htf_assessment_audit`, including effective/raw decisions,
  candle-close-based age, quality evidence, the hard-confirmation marker,
  cap, legacy veto and final result.

Historical `data_quality_valid` stays `NULL`; no value is backfilled.

## Included production code

| File | Change |
|---|---|
| `domain/TradeSignal.java` | Maps nullable `data_quality_valid`. |
| `service/AnalysisService.java` | Persists `MarketContextSnapshot.dataValid()` at signal generation. |
| `service/HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.java` | Pure FIX-154 decision matrix; returns detached effective decisions and never mutates entities. |
| `service/TradeExecutionValidationService.java` | Applies assessment to direct BUY and contextual/accumulated authority; enforces SOFT cap; records non-blocking audit evidence. |
| `service/OpportunityConsolidationService.java` | Applies the same matrix and cap to consolidation. |
| `execution/service/ExecutionIntelligenceService.java` | Keeps existing progressive raw-1h guard; invokes optional audit-only SOFT evidence after its rejection. Uses `SETUP_WAKEUP` audit path for the 5m wake-up caller. |
| `service/BuyHtfAssessmentAuditStore.java` | Snapshots and asynchronously persists best-effort Production audit rows. Replay writes no Production audit row. |
| `config/Fix154AuditAsyncConfig.java` | Dedicated one-thread, bounded executor; audit saturation drops evidence rather than borrowing a trading connection. |
| `service/Fix154Metrics.java` | Non-gating counters for untrusted 1h blocks, audit failures, SOFT caps and SOFT add blocks. |
| `service/Fix154DiagnosticsController.java` | Read-only `GET /api/fix154/health` metrics endpoint. |
| `service/Fix154RuntimeSchemaChecks.java` | Checks V107-required production and Replay schema before runtime admission. |

## Replay behavior

The regular reflection-based Replay writer persists every JPA `TradeSignal`
column. V107 therefore updates `trade_signal_test` alongside production.
FIX-154 audit rows are deliberately skipped while `ExecutionReplayScope` is
active because replay-generated entities do not have Production IDs and parity
is recorded by Replayƒ?Ts own evidence tables.

- `HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentServiceTest`
  - hard, soft, untrusted, 5m-veto precedence, 69/70 boundary, BNB #150a and
    #150b policy cases, and unknown non-bearish authority.
- `TradeExecutionValidationServiceTest`
  - direct/context SOFT caps, conservative behavior, neutral-5m exception is
    not unlocked, audit failure isolation, Production/Replay parity, and
    future-context exclusion.
- `OpportunityConsolidationServiceTest`
  - SOFT cap through consolidation.
- `BuyHtfAssessmentAuditStoreTest`
  - Replay does not write a Production audit row.
- `Fix154MigrationContractTest`
  - V107 contains production, Replay and audit schema contracts.

===== TRADING RULES =====
# FIX-154 higher-timeframe BUY rule ƒ?" 2026-10-10

- `data_quality_valid=true` is required before a bearish 1h signal can be
  treated as trusted. `NULL` is unknown and fails closed for bearish evidence.
- A trusted `STRONG_SELL` at `confidence_score >= 70` blocks the BUY.
- A trusted `SELL`, or trusted `STRONG_SELL` below 70, is a SOFT conflict:
  profiles receive effective 1h `NEUTRAL`, and any allowed initial BUY is
  capped at 25%.
- A bearish 5m still returns `5M_BEARISH_VETO` before any 1h result.
- Existing progressive/add rules retain their raw 1h requirement; a SOFT 1h
  never authorizes an add.

# FIX-153 candle continuity rule ƒ?" 2026-10-10

- Missing price evidence never becomes invented OHLCV data and never bypasses
  the data-quality gate.
- Automatic reconciliation may restore only one exact, final Binance candle.
  Its recovered/backfilled row has no close token and cannot directly cause
  analysis, a signal, or a trade.
- A later normal live close is the only route that resumes ordinary signal
  generation after a repaired quality window.


===== INVESTIGATION AGENT =====
# Trade Investigation Agent

## Role

Investigate one Crypto AI Trader trade from durable evidence. Explain what
happened, why the system entered and exited, whether it followed the intended
rules, and whether the entry/exit was supported by the information available
at that time.

Investigations are read-only. Do not change code, configuration, services,
database records, review states, Replay work, or deployment state.

## Required input

Obtain or resolve:

- Environment and application: local, test, or production.
- Database host, port, and schema.
- One trade reference: `paper_position.id`, `wallet_trade.id`, or a symbol plus
  a bounded approximate entry-time window.
- Optional question or suspected issue.

Before analysis, confirm the symbol, trade/position ID, running version or FIX
level, and ingestion mode. Read the actual schema before choosing tables or
columns. Do not assume a table is in `crypto_ai` when it may belong to
`crypto_ai_v2`.

## Evidence rules

- Use configured database and SSH/log connections without displaying secrets.
- Use bounded, read-only queries and targeted log searches.
- Treat stored timestamps as UTC; display both UTC and KSA (`UTC+03:00`).
- Attach record IDs and timestamped log evidence to every factual conclusion.
- Distinguish **confirmed fact**, **supported inference**, and **missing or
  conflicting evidence**.
- Missing logs or records do not prove that no execution/effect occurred.
- A saved signal does not prove execution. A callback return does not prove a
  transaction committed. Under FIX-153, `market_price_event.APPLIED` proves a
  committed successful protection callback, but can be a no-op when there was
  no open position; find the position/trade mutation separately.
- Current configuration does not prove which settings were active at entry. If
  no configuration history exists, report that limitation.

## Investigation workflow

### 1. Resolve identity and environment

Link the trade reference to, where available:

- `paper_position`
- `wallet_trade`
- entry and exit `trade_signal`
- `signal_processing_work`
- wallet/position execution records

Record entry and exit times in UTC and KSA. Confirm whether the trader ran in
local-candle OFF mode or a legacy shared-market mode.

### 2. Collect a small evidence bundle

Start with exact IDs and a narrow time window. Expand only when the evidence
requires it. Collect:

| Evidence | Establishes |
| --- | --- |
| `paper_position` | lifecycle, entry/exit, stop, target, status |
| `wallet_trade` | executed quantity, prices, fees, linked signal |
| `trade_signal` | decision, strategy, scores, entry eligibility, reasons |
| `signal_processing_work` | claim, attempt, completion, expiry, uncertainty |
| `candle` / `technical_indicator` | market data and analysis inputs available then |
| `market_price_event` | intra-candle protection observations and outcomes |
| configuration/version evidence | applicable rules, when historically available |
| application and timing logs | analysis, execution, protection, exceptions, waits |

Avoid fetching large signal explanation fields until they are needed.

### 3. Reconstruct the timeline

Build a timeline, explicitly showing missing stages:

`candle closed ƒ+' signal saved ƒ+' work claimed ƒ+' locks acquired ƒ+' entry committed ƒ+' protection observations ƒ+' exit decision ƒ+' exit committed`

Measure separately:

- candle close to signal generation;
- signal generation to processing/entry;
- observation time to protection completion;
- exit-trigger evidence to recorded exit.

Correlate delays only with logs/events for the same symbol, position, signal,
or price-event identity. A nearby slow SQL query is not proof of causation.

### 4. Assess entry correctness and quality

Use only evidence that was available at entry. Check:

- signal mode, closed-candle identity, freshness, and data quality;
- final decision and entry-allowed result;
- BTC context, higher-timeframe, ATR, liquidity, risk/reward, sizing, and
  strategy gates that applied at that time;
- correct signal/position linkage and absence of duplicate/stale-retry entry;
- quantity, wallet balance, risk limits, fees, and fill/reference-price gap.

Return two separate conclusions:

- **Execution correctness:** followed or violated the implemented rules.
- **Entry quality:** supported, weak, or unverifiable from contemporaneous
  evidence.

Subsequent price movement may evaluate the outcome; it must never be presented
as information the entry decision had.

### 5. Reconstruct protection and stop-loss behavior

For FIX-153, inspect accepted `LEGACY`/`APPLIED` price events and retain
`PENDING`, `NON_CANONICAL`, and `PROTECTION_NOT_APPLIED` records as evidence
gaps or exclusions. Never substitute candle OHLC for absent intra-candle price
evidence.

For each relevant accepted observation:

1. Determine the then-active stop, target, trailing stop, and profit-lock state.
2. Find the first accepted observation that met an exit threshold.
3. Confirm that protection evaluated it and identify the resulting position or
   wallet execution record.
4. Explain delays, rejections, stale/out-of-order classification, exceptions,
   lock waits, or pool pressure.

Classify the exit path accurately: fixed stop-loss, trailing stop, profit-lock,
take-profit, signal-driven exit, manual exit, or unknown.

### 6. Assess exit correctness and quality

Compare the recorded exit reason, quantity, and price with trigger evidence.
Check threshold reach, accepted-observation identity, protection timing, and
the committed execution. Calculate maximum favourable/adverse movement during
the holding period separately from the contemporaneous exit assessment.

### 7. Reconcile financial results

Recalculate using execution records:

- bought/sold quantities and weighted entry/exit prices;
- gross P&L, fees, and net P&L;
- partial exits, remaining quantity, and realized versus unrealized values.

Flag discrepancies among wallet records, position records, and UI output.

### 8. Diagnose cause and use Replay carefully

Classify findings as one or more of:

- strategy decision;
- data freshness or coverage;
- analysis delay;
- execution failure;
- protection failure;
- database, lock, or connection-pool contention;
- accounting/UI discrepancy;
- insufficient evidence.

Run Replay only as a controlled comparison with equivalent candles, accepted
prices, strategy version, and settings. State every unavailable input or
material difference. Never claim parity by substituting candle OHLC for missing
price evidence or treating `PENDING` as accepted.

## Required final report

1. **Trade identity and environment** ƒ?" IDs, symbol, version/mode, scope.
2. **UTC/KSA timeline** ƒ?" evidence-backed sequence and latency breakdown.
3. **Entry** ƒ?" execution correctness and entry quality.
4. **Exit** ƒ?" execution correctness and exit quality.
5. **Stop-loss/profit-lock** ƒ?" worked, failed, or unverifiable, with evidence.
6. **P&L reconciliation** ƒ?" quantities, prices, fees, and discrepancies.
7. **Root cause and confidence** ƒ?" confirmed defect, valid loss, configuration
   issue, delay, or insufficient evidence.
8. **Evidence references** ƒ?" record IDs, query/log timestamps, code/config
   references.
9. **Missing evidence** ƒ?" exactly what prevents a stronger conclusion.
10. **Recommended correction and regression checks** ƒ?" only when justified.

A profitable trade can violate the rules, and a losing trade can be a correct
execution. State those independently.

  43:     public Optional<PaperPosition> process(long signalId, boolean background,
  44:             Function<TradeSignal,Optional<PaperPosition>> business, Long sourceEventId) {
  45:         if(TransactionSynchronizationManager.isActualTransactionActive())
  46:             throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-127 caller must be outside the processing transaction");
  47:         if(!background && !store.exists(signalId)) {
  48:             // Automatic callers have already registered atomically with creation.
  49:             // Explicit existing-signal calls register only at the user's request.
  50:             transaction.executeWithoutResult(tx->store.register(signals.findById(signalId)
  51:                     .orElseThrow(()->new IllegalArgumentException("Signal not found: "+signalId)),ProcessingOrigin.EXPLICIT));
  52:         }
  53:         store.requireSourceOwner(signalId,sourceEventId);
  54:         for(int localAttempt=0;localAttempt<2;localAttempt++) {
  55:             var work=store.claim(signalId,background);
  56:             if(work==null) {
  57:                 log.info("[FIX-127][NOT_CLAIMED] signalId={}, background={}; no processing performed",signalId,background);
  58:                 return Optional.empty();
  59:             }
  60:             log.info("[FIX-127][ATTEMPT] signalId={}, symbol={}, interval={}, candleOpenTime={}, origin={}, attempt={}, background={}",
  61:                     signalId,work.symbol(),work.interval(),work.candleOpenTime(),work.origin(),work.attempts(),background);
  62:             java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicInteger completion=new java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicInteger(-1);
  63:             try {
  64:                 Optional<PaperPosition> result=transaction.execute(tx->{
  65:                     org.springframework.transaction.support.TransactionSynchronizationManager.registerSynchronization(
  66:                         new org.springframework.transaction.support.TransactionSynchronization() {
  67:                             @Override public void afterCompletion(int status) { completion.set(status); }
  68:                         });
  69:                     // FIX-140: observe the real transaction completion; a thrown exception alone
  70:                     // does not prove rollback. The private marker is only created here.
  71:                     if(walletCoordination!=null) {
  72:                         try { walletCoordination.symbol(work.symbol()); }
  73:                         catch(RuntimeException failure) {
  74:                             if(sourceEventId!=null && retryableSymbolLock(failure))throw new SymbolLockFailure(failure);
  75:                             throw failure;
  76:                         }
  77:                     }
  78:                     var owned=store.locked(signalId);
  79:                     if(owned==null || !"RUNNING".equals(owned.status()) || !work.owner().equals(owned.owner()))
  80:                         throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-127 processing ownership changed");
  81:                     // Acquire before PositionManagementService inserts a referencing
  82:                     // advisory or executes POSITION_STOP_LOSS. No rules evaluated yet.
  83:                     try {

  85:                     } catch(RuntimeException ex) {

  88:                     }

  99:                     if(!freshness.eligible(work))throw new Expired();

 107:             } catch(Expired ex) {

 137:     // FIX-140: classify only known lock cancellation/deadlock errors. Communications
 138:     // errors are deliberately excluded even when nested below a data-access exception.
 139:     static boolean retryableSymbolLock(Throwable failure) {
 140:         for(Throwable e=failure;e!=null;e=e.getCause())
 141:             if(e instanceof java.sql.SQLException sql && sql.getSQLState()!=null && sql.getSQLState().startsWith("08"))return false;

 146:         return false;
 147:     }

 151:     // A private marker cannot be supplied by a later business callback.

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** `paper_position.closed_at` is set from application `Instant.now()` in both signal and live-protection close paths. It is a recorded close/persistence time, not the Binance observation time. A delay claim requires the matching accepted price event, wallet SELL, and audit evidence—not a timestamp comparison alone.
- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** The current live-protection path attempts the mechanical wallet exit before closing `paper_position`; the current signal path saves the paper close before invoking the wallet exit. The latter runs inside the coordinator’s transaction, so source order alone does not prove a committed paper/wallet divergence. Records are required.

### DB_REQUEST

QueryId: DBQ-CODEX-I7-001  
Purpose: Establish the physical authoritative position schema, retrieve only the six frozen positions, and detect any present-day drift from the frozen negative-closed selection. Results must treat all timestamps as UTC; this request does not claim to recreate the original snapshot.

SQL:
```sql
SHOW CREATE TABLE paper_position;

SELECT *
FROM paper_position
WHERE id IN (182, 172, 162, 158, 143, 137)
ORDER BY FIELD(id, 182, 172, 162, 158, 143, 137);

SELECT id, status, closed_at, realized_pnl
FROM paper_position
WHERE status = 'CLOSED'
  AND closed_at IS NOT NULL
  AND realized_pnl < 0
ORDER BY closed_at DESC
LIMIT 6;
```
